# Phase 4 history — finished work moved out of PHASE4.md (2026-10-30)

`SelectionBlob` list (Select_Box_Scale × Scale_Factor, own selected units, hidden while targeting/cinematic; material from type / faction callback). `build_hud_type_table(GameObjectTypes)`. **Shell** (`tactical_hud.hpp`): selected-unit panel (selection grouped by type, s_select/s_health/s_shield, pads per faction, count text), radar frame + team-colour blips, credits text, [port] command-bar/radar stand-in frames, hit boxes (topmost layer), portrait click → `commands::ev::select(ids)` (HUD stays read-only). **Input** (`hud_input.hpp`): `InputRouter` – press order overlay → targeting → dialogs → chat → HUD → world (middle never HUD), world-owned LMB/RMB releases always reach the world, HUD-owned presses never reach it, wheel/keys to dialogs only while focused. **GPU** (`hud_renderer.hpp`): one dynamic-rendering pass on the image `Renderer::render` leaves in `color_after` (after the whole post chain), colour LOAD + depth CLEAR (step 4), 3 blend pipelines, 16 texture pages (white, atlas, glyph pages re-uploaded in-frame), per-frame-slot buffers/descriptors, step callback for shell models (`hud_ortho_view` + `Renderer::draw`); `selection_blob.hpp`: space = unit-quad model with the material effect (BlobStencilMasked.fx, Texture0/1) as a main-list Instance (Transparent bucket when effects load), land = `terrain::ShadowBlob` for `MapScene::set_shadow_blobs`; `hud_sim_bridge.hpp` (header-only) InterpolatedFrame → HudUnit. MSVC, clang 20: 0 warnings in engine/ui, the 4 ui tests pass (AMD RX 9070 XT, validation required, 0 messages). **Stage 2 (2026-10-27)**: **Shells** (`hud_shell.hpp`, alamo_assets): real i_tactical_controls.alo / i_galactic_controls.alo / i_main_reinforce.alo read — model units = canvas units, +Y up, +Z towards the viewer (bars z 5 > portraits z 4 > borders z 1), but the **model origin is the anchored screen corner**, not the canvas centre (tactical bar bones x 0..1012, y −72..309 = bottom-left; reinforcement window y −270..0 = top-left) → stage-1 assumption corrected: `shell_anchor_offset` puts the origin on the edge the bones extend away from on a Model_Offset axis, canvas centre otherwise; every component bone has a hidden placeholder quad = its rectangle (s_select 50×50, radar 175×175, special_border 54×119) → `ComponentPlacement::size/rect_center` for hit boxes; visible art = per-faction `<name>_ALT<n>` meshes (faceplates ALT0 Empire / 1 Rebel / 2 Underworld). `ShellSet` (load through a model loader, bones → `HudLayout`, per-faction mesh masks, visibility = shell activity); `ShellDrawer` (alamo_ui_draw: upload via `Renderer::upload_model`, instances = translation by `shell_offset` + mesh mask, drawn at ShellModels half 0 with `hud_ortho_view(kHudCameraShells)` and a swapped-in HUD lighting, restored after). **Buttons** (`hud_buttons.hpp`): `HudButtons` runtime per component (hidden/disabled default from data, selected, flash, blink, icon/text overrides, two dials, overlays), visual Disabled > Pressed > Selected > MouseOver > Normal, Toggle, clicks with Shift/Ctrl, `HudSfxHook` (Mouse_Over_SFX on hover enter, Click_SFX / Right_Click_SFX) for P3; `TooltipState` (Tooltip_Delay 330 ms from GameConstants, Tooltip_Text[state], tooltip_back + faction frame, layer 10, wrapped, kept on screen); `parse_dialog_tooltips` (GUIDialogs.xml `<Tooltips>`). **Hotkeys** (`hotkeys.hpp`): the 153-slot table of UI.md §4.3 with names, profile load/save (KeySettingsCount / KeySettingKeycode<n> / KeySettingModifier<n>), bind removes duplicates and moves the derived Ctrl/Shift/Alt slots of 0..9, exact-modifier matching, `HotkeyDispatcher` → ControlGroup recall / Ctrl assign / Shift add (assign+add bits), SelectAll, Stop (needs a selection), Retreat + `HotkeyEvent`s for the rest, 1 s double recall = camera jump; `apply_to_bindings` syncs P1 `Bindings`; `OrderTranslator::set_keyboard_orders(false)` hands the keyboard to the dispatcher. **Panels** (`tactical_hud.hpp`): `HudProviders` callbacks (abilities per selected group until S9, build queue, reinforcements); ability buttons special_button_NN (distinct ability types across the selection, users merged, recharge dial i_button_sa_recharge, active = selected look, autofire overlay) + special_border_NN full/left/centre/right pieces per used column; tactical build queue tqueueNN (progress dial, count) → click = `Production` cancel; land reinforcements r_RRCC → `HudAction::BeginReinforcement` + `reinforce_event(unit, point)`; generic buttons (c_buttonNN, b_*) from the shell bones; ability click → `SetUnitAbilityMode` toggle or `HudAction::BeginAbilityTargeting`, right click → `SetAbilityAutofire`; b_retreat → Retreat; other buttons → `HudAction::Button`; portrait Shift-click drops the group, Ctrl-click keeps its first unit. `TacticalHud::update_input(RoutedInput, …)` replaces the stage-1 click path (kept as `on_pointer`). **Radar** (`hud_radar.hpp`): RadarMap.xml (events, backdrops, FOW colours, passability palettes with Default inheritance), `RadarImage` (terrain RGBA or passability classes + FOW visibility grid composited, generation-tracked) uploaded as dynamic HUD page 2 (`HudRenderer::set_dynamic_page`; glyph pages now start at 3), blips (selected colorized 209,255,209), `RadarEvents` pings, camera footprint, left press/drag = `RadarCameraJump`, right click = MoveToPosition + Default_Click ping. **Order feedback** (`hud_markers.hpp`): waypoint lines (dotted, projected), `MoveAcks` (GameConstants GUI_*_Command_Ack_Effect / scale land 1 / space 5 / radar event → `MoveAckRequest` for the particle host + a screen ring), hard-point reticles (`ReticleTable` from the HardPoint_Target_Reticle_* pairs, 0.03 × screen height, layer 1). **Text**: `wrap_text` / `add_text_block` (Max_Text_Width, 95 % at 800 px). discovery `<model dir>\<base>_<SUFFIX>_<NN>.ALA` per id, NN from 00 until the first gap, base = model name or Land_/Space_Model_Anim_Override_Name; bound only when model bones = ALA bones + 1 (track → mini 0x05 bone), otherwise kept (frame count / fps / variant count are sim-visible) but posed as the bind pose. **State** `AnimWorld`: SoA rows by ObjectId, three slots (primary / previous / overlay: type, variant, mode, frame, speed, timed timer), crossfade duration / remaining / weight, lock bit, idle timer. `play()` = 0x3a92f0 + 0x265560 (lock, 0xFFFF random variant on the passed RNG, type −1 clear, blend start / keep-previous at weight ≤ 0.25 / modes 2-3 rescale / hard cut, start frame, timed); `create()` = model-created rule (idle, Loop_Idle_Anim_00, Idle_Anim_00_Rate_Mod, random start frame only when a clip exists); `advance(frame, pf)` at t = frame/30, pure per row: loops wrap at count − 1 by repeated subtraction, one-shots stop at count − 2, timed slots expire, weight = 1 − remaining/duration; `service_idle` (variant [1, n−1], timer + [210, 450]); `switch_preserving_phase`; pose = bind / one clip / crossfade / overlay (non-excluded bones), nlerp normalised with dmath::fast_rsqrt_retail, local × parent world, billboard modes 6/7 copy the parent; | R4 | Particle systems (GPU) | ✅ 2026-10-27 (render side; sim PARTICLE objects done in S10-int (TacticalBattle spawns them); Lua `Attach_Particle_Effect` bound (L1 presentation log entry ObjectAttachParticleEffect) but no host consumes the entry yet; marked R4 hooks in engine/render: `SceneHook` + `Renderer::set_scene_hook` (renderer.hpp) and its 3 call sites in `Renderer::render` (renderer.cpp), `add_subdirectory(particles)` (CMakeLists.txt), `--particles` dispatch (app/main.cpp) | ANIM_PARTICLES.md §B. **Data** (`particle_data.hpp`): particle ALO → `SystemDef` through alamo_formats: texture names without directories, lifetime shape recomputed from 0x0F/0x13, 7 tracks resampled to 32 entries (mode 2 = step, 0/1 linear), links (−1 = none; one parent per child, child pool × parent pool), B.4 pool size + 5,003 cap, reach; **track keys are (value, time 0..1) with u32 values on the byte tracks** (survey of all 1,490 emitters; SPEC 3.3 and alamo_formats named them (time 0..100, value); both corrected in R-int); `ParticleLibrary` (case-insensitive, misses cached); `parse_proxy_name` (_ALTn / _LODn index, name cut at the first). **CPU bookkeeping** (`particle_sim.hpp`, `ParticleWorld`): update(d) with d = 0.03 s per sim tick (`tick_delta`, ≤ 1 s, 0 = frozen); per emitter age, interval events (burst delay or 1/rate, per-event 0x26 or 1) back-filled with pre-age = now − event time and lerp of the system transform, events older than the lifetime skipped, burst count, initial delay, stop time, pre-warm (0.1 s steps past skip time), emission-scale test, free-slot stack (spawn dropped when empty), Particle Detail slot spread ((0xEDF1 + i·0xECF3) mod pool < ceil(scale₂·detail·pool), re-evaluated on change), per-slot age/life so deaths match the GPU's float additions, spawn-on-death / during-life child schedules per parent slot, weather pools filled once with random ages, leave-particles release, systems kill themselves when every emitter is done; free RNG of its own; one `UpdatePacket` per update (range clears, emitters with prev/cur transform + velocity, 32-byte spawn records). **GPU** (`particle_gpu.hpp`, `ParticleRenderer : SceneHook`): persistent 64-byte particle records in one buffer (range allocator), per packet CSClear / CSUpdate (age, death → death-position buffer, gravity on z, accel (object-space rotated), outward accel, wind ½·d, spin from track 6, weather wrap) / CSSpawn (shapes point/box/cube/sphere/cylinder, outward speed, inherit velocity, closed-form pre-age step, random colour / rotation / spin direction, linked children at the parent particle or its death position); draw list: visible systems with parent fade > 0.01, conservative AABB (emitter-position trail over the lifetime + reach) vs frustum, back-to-front per system, emitters in file order, one instanced draw each (6 vertices × pool); bitonic CSSort (one 256-thread group, 8,192 keys of 19-bit far-first distance) for blend 0/2/5/11 with 0x2B; camera-facing quads with rotation, tails along the screen-plane velocity, atlas cell from track 5, colour/alpha/scale tracks + random colour; blend 0 opaque z-write, 1/4 ONE/ONE (alpha fades RGB), 3/6 ZERO/SRCCOLOR, others SRCALPHA/INVSRCALPHA; buckets 8 (Transparent), 7 (Water, system flag) and 11 (Heat, heat emitters drawn as PrimAlpha into the heat target); textures through `AssetCache::texture` (built-in soft dot for missing ones). **Attachment** (`particle_attach.hpp`, `ProxyAttachments`): per owner key and ALO proxy: stripped name → system at owner × proxy bone (Instance::bone_world or bind pose), updated while hidden; draw gates = the proxy's HardpointVisibility sub-object bit (meshes, proxies, hardpoints) else ALO 07, alt filter, _LOD proxies at the model's LOD, fade; finished one-shots not recreated; owners not synced released; `sync_static` (map placements / sky, key = hash(model uid, transform)); `sync_effect` (owner whose model is a particle file = sim PARTICLE objects); `sync_sim_frame` (R1 InterpolatedFrame, key = object id, builder's hardpoint mask, optional R3 pose source). **App** `alamo_render --particles` (showcase grid of shipped or `--synthetic` systems, `--dump`), `--particles --map NAME` (MapScene placements + sky with their proxies, lit frame graph), `--particles --sim-check` (synthetic sim map, proxies on every object, particles on vs off). **Results**: FoC 179 files / 471 emitters, EaW 409 / 1,019, 0 failures (blend FoC 1:327 2:144, EaW 1:588 2:410 11:21; heat 47 / 127; linked 4 / 24; 3 / 6 pools capped); FoC+EaW models: 5,685 proxies, 2,206 resolve only after stripping (doc: same), 3,347 as written, 132 nothing (doc 3,345 / 134); per-tick CRC logs identical with particles on vs off (synthetic map, 300 ticks, 168 systems / 124,320 spawned, on MSVC, clang 20); Corellia: 206 proxies (84 stripped, 1 unresolved), 204 systems; Byss: 437 proxies (372 stripped), 412 systems, heat draws in bucket 11; 0 validation messages everywhere. | N1 | Save / load + replay: stage 1 galactic-map saves, stage 2 saves during a tactical battle, alamo_game save / load menus, save size | ✅ 2026-10-29 (stage 1, then stage 2 + menus + size the same day; in-game check of the menus waits on Gabriel) | Stage 2: engine/tactical (movement, fog, tree, combat, abilities, garrison, hangar: src/*_save.cpp), engine/ai/tactical (src/tactical_save.cpp), engine/anim (src/anim_save.cpp), engine/lua (PresentationLog), engine/apps/sim (src/tactical_battle_save.cpp, save_io.hpp, campaign_battles, sim_anim, story_session), engine/apps/headless (save_run, skirmish_run); menus: engine/apps/game (save_games.*, game_session, app/game_main.cpp), engine/ui (gui_menus); size: engine/core (compress.hpp/.cpp, save_file), engine/ai (ai_lua_runtime, galactic_ai_save). Stage 1: engine/core (save_file.hpp/.cpp, ByteWriter/Reader save helpers), engine/objects (ObjectStore), engine/commands (scheduler, events, recording), engine/galactic (src/galactic_save.cpp), engine/ai (src/ai_save*.cpp, src/galactic_ai_save.cpp + every AI system), engine/lua (src/vm_persist.cpp, 2 Lua patches, bridge / players / fog / cinematic write+read), engine/apps/sim (save_session.*, sim_host_save.cpp), engine/apps/headless (save_run.*); build_agent.cmd | **Design**: snapshot saves. Every system has `write(ByteWriter&)` / `read(ByteReader&)` with a u32 version first; a failed read leaves the system unchanged; definitions are rebuilt from the same data and hooks re-attached by the host. **File** `SaveFile` ("ALSV"): header, named chunks (names = CrcRegistry system names), trailing StateHasher digest; every single-byte corruption and every truncation is rejected. **Chunks**: session, frame, objects, galactic.state, players, rng, commands, globals, story, story.lua, story.bridge, story.players, galactic.conflicts, battles, ai (anim and the tactical side of FogScriptCells / CinematicTransports wait for stage 2). **Lua**: whole VMs saved and resumed, including threads paused in Sleep loops; all 370 shipped FoC scripts save, reload to identical bytes and keep running identically. Two Lua 5.0.2 patches found by the work: (1) dead keys of freed objects are marked removed, so table traversal no longer depends on allocator address reuse (a determinism bug in normal play too; twin-VM test with allocator noise fails without it); (2) values reachable through open upvalues of dead coroutines stay alive (use-after-free; Lua 5.1 behaviour). No existing golden moved. **Commands**: `SavePoint`, `CommandRecording::from_save`, `CommandPipeline::replay_from_save`. **Host**: SimHost `save` / `load` / `replay_after_load` / `record_after_load`; alamo_headless `--save-session synthetic\|synthetic-nostory\|hotm\|galactic --save-at T FILE`, `--load FILE [--replay STREAM]`, `--save-check`. **Check** (save at T, run on, compare every tick's CRC): in-process load, fresh-process load, replay of the stream recorded after the load and of the original tick-0 stream are all identical; a session with another seed is refused. Synthetic story session: save at 450, 12 chunks, 751 ticks identical; Heart of the Maw galactic with AI: save at 1800, 11 chunks, run to 5400. The 3 test saves are byte-identical between MSVC and clang. Size: the galactic save with AI was 15.4 MB, 15.3 MB of it the AI Lua VMs (fixed below: 150 KB). Tests: alamo_core_save_tests, alamo_galactic_save_tests, alamo_lua_save_tests, additions to the objects / commands / ai / sim tests, alamo_headless_save_synthetic / _synthetic_nostory / _hotm. Also: tools/lint/check_sim_math.cmake reads files as UTF-8 (a "§" in a comment split a line: false positive). Done by 5 parallel agents (n1-objects, n1-galactic, n1-ai, n1-host, n1-lua), each in its own build tree via `build_agent.cmd`. **Stage 2, saves during a tactical battle** (6 agents n2-*, 2026-10-29): every tactical system has write / read (MovementSystem incl. hyperspace / engines, TreeSet saved as its entry set and rebuilt (query results shape-independent), FogOfWar saved whole (a recount after load would double-count), CombatSystem, WeatherSystem, AbilitySystem, SpecialWeaponSystem, garrison / capture, hangar / contents / production / engine loops, TacticalAiWorld (+ `relink()` after read), AnimWorld / AnimBehaviors, PresentationLog). New host chunks "spatial", "story.presentation", battle.movement / combat / abilities / garrison / hangar / fog / damage / state / ai (registered by CampaignBattles while a battle runs), real "anim" (no model re-created and no synced draw after a load), "story" v2 (tactical plot set), "battles" v2 (battle in flight). **Rebuild on load**: when the save is deeper than the rebuilt session, SimHost::load reads the bottom modes, calls the new `SaveParticipant::rebuild` (CampaignBattles loads the map and pushes the battle as at a normal start), then reads everything. save_allowed refuses only: no session, a tactical mode nothing can rebuild, a battle ended / stopped for auto-resolve (clears next tick). Headless: `--save-session skirmish --campaign synthetic\|foc\|foc-land`, `--battles auto\|fight\|fight-all[+anim]`, `--story`, `--save-when any\|battle\|galactic\|pending`; the check also compares the synced RNG after the load. New tests, all identical every tick in-process, fresh-process and both replays: alamo_headless_save_hotm_anim, _skirmish_foc (saved in battle at 600, +2400 ticks), _skirmish_foc_land (900, +2700), _hotm_battle (story on, saved in a running battle), _hotm_before_battle, _skirmish_anim. Module tests save mid-battle and continue identically (e.g. combat 420 ticks / 289 shots, tactical AI 900–2550 frames, movement on 4 threads vs serial). **Menus (t09)**: alamo_game Save (in-game menu; disabled with the reason when save_allowed refuses or a chunk would be empty), Load (main and in-game menu; loaded into a new session first, the current game kept on failure), Delete, overwrite / delete confirmations; saves `%LOCALAPPDATA%\alamo\Saves\<name>.alsv`, label "name - Title (Faction), week N, date"; descriptor kinds game.campaign / game.skirmish. alamo_game_save_tests (FoC): Heart of the Maw campaign at 300, Kuat space and Alderaan land skirmishes saved mid-battle with animation on, each loaded in a fresh process through the menu path, identical. **Size (t10)**: unused (pristine) AI plan VMs saved as a digest of the reader's own copy, plus an in-house LZ77 + Huffman codec (engine/core compress, 16 MiB window) over the SaveFile body (format v2; v1 refused): HotM 15.4 MB -> 150 KB, load 140 -> 80 ms, save 88 -> 105 ms (first save); size guards in test_ai_galactic / test_save_file. **Open**: in-game check of the menus (Gabriel: save, overwrite, delete, load from both menus, save in a battle); late-game and mid-mission story save size unmeasured (early Underworld campaign about 50 KB, guard alamo_ai_story_save_size); human-fought campaign land battle save untested (space battle covered through save_game / load_game in alamo_game_save_tests); tactical AI decision_log uncapped (not saved). **Closed 2026-10-30 (n1f)**: the "battle pending" branch cannot be reached by a human conflict (only start_map_battle between ticks; tested that way, plus the real Vote states); skirmish save label shows battle time; tactical AI command bus drained each update; a private PresentationLog (presentation without attach_presentation) is not saved |

  - ✅ (1) ships sideways (2026-10-29, ships agent). Two causes: (a) `MovementSystem::commit` wrote the yaw in degrees into `facing.z` (the store facing is a direction vector everywhere else), so every moved unit was drawn pitched almost vertical (land infantry lay flat, ships stood on end) and anim fire bones used the stale spawn yaw; now it writes (cos, sin, 0). (b) Alamo models face model -Y (left +X, up +Z: muzzle / engine / hardpoint bones of ships, vehicles, fighters, missiles, infantry feet), but every facing -> matrix assumed +X forward (90° yaw). One convention now: facing = the nose; `render::object_transform` puts model -Y along it (`kIdentityFacing` = (0,-1,0) for as-authored draws: galactic planets, starbases, portrait, corruption effects), the sim fire-bone matrices (sim_anim, tactical_battle) and `bt` bone positions match, `BattleCatalog` converts hardpoint bones to the combat frame (x forward), map placements get facing Rz(yaw)·(0,-1,0) (map_spawn, bt markers) so props / structures keep their placement transform.   - ✅ playtest 3 t10 / t11 (2026-10-29, pt3-render agent). **t10** soft shadows darkened the whole land frame: `StencilDarkenFinalBlur` multiplies the frame by the blurred back-buffer ALPHA (alpha 0 = shadowed) and the port's passes write alpha (terrain / unit texture alpha, water, decorations), so every pixel with alpha < 1 took the shadow colour (the original keeps unshadowed alpha at 1 with RGB-only writes, TERRAIN_WATER_POST §4.3). Fix in `Renderer::render` / `post_darken(reset_alpha)`: the soft path first writes alpha 1 over the frame (StencilDarkenToAlpha pass without its stencil test, own prim vertex block) and then the alpha-0 darken; real _MP_LAND_ENDOR frame, mean luma off 193.2 / hard 192.2 / soft 108.2 before -> 192.2 after, 98 % of pixels darker than hard before -> 0.09 %. The checkbox could only turn shadows off because `apply_detail_options` never set `RecordOptions::soft_shadows` (the view's own value, false, stayed); it now sets `soft_shadows = checkbox && shadows`. Tests: new `alamo_render_shadow_tests` (real map shadows off / hard / soft) + `alamo_render_tests` case (a2: ground with vertex alpha 100, soft lit pixel 200 vs 139 without the fix). **t11** bound to real parameters (sliders 0..100, 100 / default = old look; mappings are [M] guesses, documented in game_display.hpp): Shader detail -> `RecordOptions::shader_detail` 0..3 (RENDER §1.5 / §6: 0 = no shadow volumes, hard darken; < 3 caps water at 1, < 1 at 0); Water detail -> `water_detail` 0..2 (2 reflection + refraction targets, 1 reflection only, 0 none; targets not recorded this frame are no longer bound); Texture detail -> `Renderer::set_texture_reduction` (0..2 top mips skipped via sampler minLod, same latch as anisotropy, scene samplers only); Particle detail -> `ParticleWorld::set_detail` (slider / 100, ANIM_PARTICLES B.4) through `GalacticView` / `BattleView::set_particle_detail`; Environment detail -> battle Object_Fade_Begin / End scale 0.6..1.0; Gamma slider (50 = identity, 2^((s-50)/50)) -> new `Renderer::apply_gamma` (src/gamma.cpp, shaders/gamma.hlsl) pass over the finished frame incl.
